HISTORY AND DEVELOPMENT                                                       歷史沿革
1962   I Lan Foods Industrial Co., Ltd. was established in May 1962,          於1962年5月成立宜蘭食品工業股份有限
       which manufactured canned agricultural products mainly for             公司，該公司當時從事製造罐頭農產品並
       export.                                                                以出口外銷業務為主。
1983   In 1983, we collaborated with Iwatsuka Confectionery Co., Ltd.         於1983年，我們與日本領先的米果製造商
       (“ICCL”), one of the leading Japanese rice cracker producers, to       之一的岩塚制果株式會社（「岩塚制果」）合
       jointly develop the rice cracker market in Taiwan region. Since        作，攜手開拓台灣地區米果市場。自1983
       1983, we have been producing and marketing our products under          年起，我們開始生產及營銷「旺旺」品牌的
       the “Want Want” brand.                                                 產品。
1989   We introduced the “Want Want” brand in the Chinese mainland in         1989年「旺旺」品牌進入中國境內市場。
       1989.
1992   We ventured into the Chinese mainland and established our first        於1992年進軍中國境內並在湖南省設立首
       subsidiary in Hunan province in the Chinese mainland in 1992. In       間位於中國境內的附屬公司。此後數年，
       the following years, we grew from a pure rice cracker company to       我們成功從一家單一米果公司發展為多元
       a diversified food and beverages company.                              化的食品及飲料公司。
1996   In May 1996, Want Want Holdings Ltd. (“WWHL”), our subsidiary,         我們的附屬公司旺旺控股有限公司（「旺旺
       was listed on the Main Board of Singapore Exchange Securities          控股」）於1996年5月在新加坡證券交易所
       Trading Limited (“SGX-ST”) and subsequently diversified into the       有限公司（「新交所」）主板上市。其後，更
       hospital, hotel and property businesses and other investments.         將業務作多元化發展至醫院、酒店及地產
                                                                              業務以及其他投資項目。
2007   WWHL delisted from SGX-ST in September 2007 and conducted              旺旺控股於2007年9月在新交所除牌。隨
       a group restructuring which involved (i) the incorporation of Want     後進行重組，包括(i)成立中國旺旺控股有
       Want China Holdings Limited (the “Company”) as the new holding         限公司（「本公司」）為其所有食品及飲料業
       company of our core operations related to the food and beverages       務的新投資控股公司；(ii)把醫院、酒店及
       businesses; (ii) the divestment and transfer of the hospital, hotel    地產業務以及其他投資項目剝離並於2007
       and property businesses and other investments to San Want              年12月31日轉移至San Want Holdings
       Holdings Limited (“San Want”) on 31 December 2007, and the             Limited（「神旺」），再透過向當時旺旺控股
       interests in San Want were distributed to the then shareholders of     股東派發神旺權益的實物股息而剝離。
       WWHL by way of dividend in specie.
2008   Our Company was listed on the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong              於2008年3月26日，本公司正式在香港聯
       Limited (the “HK Stock Exchange”) on 26 March 2008 (the                合交易所有限公司（「香港聯交所」）上市

PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES                                                          主要業務
The principal activities of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively    本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「旺旺」、「我
referred to as “Want Want”, “We” or the “Group”) are the manufacturing,       們」或「集團」）之主要業務為米果、乳品及
distribution and sale of rice crackers, dairy products and beverages, snack   飲料、休閒食品及其他產品之製造、分銷
foods and other products.                                                     及銷售。

KEY MARKETS                                                                   主要市場
Most of our operations are located in the People’s Republic of China          我們的業務大部份位於全球增長速度最快
(“PRC”), which is one of the fastest growing economies in the world. We       經濟體之一的中華人民共和國（「中國」）。
have an extensive nationwide sales and distribution network throughout        我們在中國的全國性銷售及分銷網絡龐
the PRC. We also export our products to other markets, including North        大，我們也出口產品至其他市場：例如北
America, East Asia, South East Asia and Europe.                               美、東亞、東南亞及歐洲。

                                                                             Corporate Culture



「不忘初心唱響旺旺」社歌口號大賽
“Don’t Forget Our Original Aspiration
and Sing for Want Want” Corporate
Song and Slogan Competition
集團秉持「緣、自信、大團結」的經營理念，堅持「合心、合
氣、合力、合作」的目標。為加強員工和經銷商的凝聚力、
向心力，提升集團精神面貌，特別策劃舉辦「不忘初心 唱響
旺旺」社歌口號大賽活動。
The Group adheres to the business philosophy of “People-
Oriented, Self-Confidence and Unity” and insists on
achieving the goal of “Undivided Commitment, Harmony,
Joint-Efforts and Cooperation”. In order to strengthen
the cohesion and centripetal force of employees and
distributors, and to boost the spirit of the Group, we
specially planned and held the “Don’t Forget Our Original
Aspiration and Sing for Want Want” corporate song and
slogan competition.

                                                             旺旺精神
                                                    Spirit of Want Want



      經營理念：
                         自信 笑口由己 誠心               大團結 左手擁抱
     緣 頭圓渥髮




旺仔的頭形是圓，代表「投緣」。頭部好像      旺仔笑口常開代表充滿自信。       旺仔的雙手展開作擁抱狀，左
剛洗完髮，髮未乾，故有握髮之稱。「握       你的自信來自你周全的準備，       手表示大團結。如果策略是對
髮」出自《周公誡子》中，「一沐三握髮，      當你充滿自信時，一定可以充       的，會因團結而創造無限美好
一飯三吐哺，猶恐失天下之士」。所以握       滿笑容。自信是我們做事的第       的前景，即使策略是失敗的，
髮也表示有延攬賢士的意思。            一基礎。笑口中舌如心形，代       也會因團結而使損失降到最
董事長的話：公司非常惜緣惜才，公司就       表著我們赤誠的心：對待上級       低。在一個大家庭裏，大家務
像一個大家庭，大家有緣在一起，應該盡       全心服從愛戴，對待下屬關心       必心連心，手連手，團結在一
我們的職責，珍惜這個家庭，亦即你心中       倍至，對待客戶誠心誠意，必       起。
有我，我心中有你，這才是真正惜緣。        能處處逢源、事半功倍。




    經營指標：                                         開創空前新事業
  世界聚龍 立志雄心                                         奮袂而起




旺仔張開雙臂有招攏聚集之                                 旺仔挽起的袖手及褲子，表示
意。大家有緣相聚在一起，為                                呼應空前新事業，並勤奮努
共同的目標創造更多騰飛的                                 力。我們立志雄心成為「世界
龍，隨著事業的發展，一條                                 聚龍」，年年都將會有新的事
條騰飛的龍滙聚在這個大家庭                                業體，為了開創新事業，大家

Chairman’s Statement




Dear shareholders:                                                                尊敬的各位股東：


The COVID-19 pandemic, which occurred in early 2020, has caused global            2020 年初這場造成全球動盪不安的疫情，
disruption and has, indeed, affected some of our original planning. While         的確影響了我們原本的一些計劃，旺旺全
doing our best at undertaking different precautions, our employees and we         體員工除了做好自身防護外，也善盡企業
were also committed to fulfil our corporate social responsibility by donating     社會責任，積極捐贈各項防疫物資及公司
various pandemic prevention supplies and products of the Company and              產品，全力支援旺旺客戶及供應商共度艱
provide our utmost supports to our customers and suppliers during this            難時刻。另外，在各級政府幫助下，我們
difficult time. With the help of different levels of government, we endeavored    也以最快速度於 2020 年 3 月份陸續全面復
to gradually resume the operation of all our production facilities and offices    工復產，我要在此表達最深感謝，並也借
in March 2020. I hereby express my heartfelt thanks to all who joined in          此機會再次向偉大的一線抗擊疫情的人員
the effort and, using this opportunity, I would once again like to show my        獻上最高敬意。
greatest respect to all frontline personnel who have been fighting against
the virus.


Due to the adverse impact caused by the pandemic on the fourth quarter            2019 財年雖在最後季度受到疫情波及，導
of 2019FY, revenue in the 2019FY decreased by 3.0% to RMB20,094.5                 致 收 益 較 2018 財 年 下 滑 3.0% 至 人 民 幣
million as compared with that of 2018FY. Benefiting from the product-             200.945 億，毛利率則受惠於產品組合優
mix optimisation and the decrease in cost of certain raw materials, our           化及部分原材料成本下降，較 2018 財年
gross profit margin nevertheless grew from 45.4% in 2018FY to 48.0%.              45.4% 成長至 48.0%，營運利潤也較 2018
Operating profit also increased by 4.8% as compared with that of 2018FY           財年增長 4.8% 至 48.059 億人民幣。最後，
to RMB4,805.9 million. Lastly, net profit attributable to equity holders of the   歸屬於本公司權益持有人淨利潤較 2018 財
Company was RMB3,649.2 million, representing a 5.0% increase over that            年增長 5.0% 至 36.492 億人民幣。
of 2018FY.

                                                                                              Chairman’s Statement




The COVID-19 pandemic has underscored the importance of multiple                    這次疫情中我們也看到了許多渠道及數位
channels and digital platforms. It has also created many market opportunities       化平臺的重要，同時也孕育了許多市場機
that are waiting for us to explore. At a time filled with uncertainties, we learn   會等待我們去探索。在這個充滿不確定的
more about ourselves and make the very most of our potentials. I requested          時代中，我們確實認識自己，進而絕對發
our management to regard the year of 2020 as a “year of learning”. Only by          揮自己。我要求旺旺管理層把 2020 年定義
remaining humble and continuing to learn new knowledge and new ideas at             為「學習年」，惟有每日每刻不斷地謙卑學
every moment could Want Want keep progressing forward steadfastly in the            習新知，才能使旺旺不管遭遇多少艱辛與
face of different hardships and challenges ahead of us and to grow with our         挑戰都能昂首闊步，堅定向前，並且與我
customers, and to keep abreast with new development!                                們的客戶一同攜手成長，與時俱進！

報告重點概覽
Overview of key points of the report

Reporting Scope                                                                     報告範圍
During the reporting period, more than 90% of the Group’s revenue and               本集團報告期內超過90%的收益及業務均
businesses were generated and conducted in the Chinese mainland, so                 在中國境內發生，故此報告的數據及信息
this report sets out the data and information generated in the Chinese              均為本集團在中國境內發生之數據信息。
mainland exclusively. The reporting period refers to the period from 1 April        報告的時間範圍為2019年4月1 日至2020
2019 to 31 March 2020 (“reporting period” or “2019FY”).                             年3月31日（「報告期」或「2019財年」）。


References                                                                          報告準則

and Governance Reporting Guide” (“ESG Reporting Guide”) as set out                  港聯交所」）證券上市規則附錄27《環境、

Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited” issued by The Stock Exchange                   告倡議組織《可持續發展報告標準》。本報
of Hong Kong Limited (“HKEx”) and with reference to the “Sustainability             告期內，我們已遵守《環境、社會及管治
Reporting Standards” issued by Global Reporting Initiative (“GRI”). During          報告指引》所載的「不遵守就解釋」條文規
the reporting period, we acted in accordance with the “comply or explain”           定。
provisions in the “ESG Reporting Guide”.

strictly by the “Food Recall Management Measures” and other laws and              品召回管理辦法》等法律法規，建立了規
regulations, and establish a standardised simulation traceability system          範的模擬追溯體系和完善的召回流程。從
and a complete recall process. From food safety risk identification and           食品安全的風險識別與判定，到制定召回
judgement to making recall announcements, implementing recalls, and               公告、實施召回工作、及時改善產品，旺
improving products in a timely manner, the Group achieves full process            旺做到全過程控制，保證產品質量。模擬
control to ensure product quality. The simulation traceability system makes       追溯體系利用ERP數據庫，對產品生產
use of ERP database to monitor the production and sales process of the            及銷售過程進行監控，實現原料、成品雙
product, and realises two-way traceability of raw materials and finished          向追溯切實有效。全國各生產基地，每年
products. All production bases in the country simulate the recall once a          開展1次模擬召回演練，兩次成品至原物
year, and conducts two-way simulation traceability from finished products         料、原物料至成品的雙向模擬追溯，報告
to raw materials and from raw materials to finished products twice a year.        期內，追溯總耗時<4小時，平均追溯時效

and stable labour relations can be built and developed.

FOREIGN EXCHANGE RISKS                                                          外匯風險
The presentation currency of the Group is RMB, but the Company’s                本集團呈列貨幣為人民幣，但本公司的功
functional currency is still USD. More than 90% of our operating activities     能貨幣仍為美元，而本集團 90% 以上經營
are conducted in the Chinese mainland. Our Chinese mainland subsidiaries’       活動在中國境內，中國境內附屬公司的功
functional currency is RMB. The Group’s foreign exchange risks arise mainly     能貨幣為人民幣。本集團的外匯風險主要
from procurement of raw materials and equipment from overseas, overseas         源自境外的原物料採購、設備採購和海外
dividend payments and certain recognised assets and liabilities.                股利支付，及若干已確認資產或負債。


As procurement of raw materials and equipment from overseas and USD             由於本集團的境外原物料、設備採購及美
denominated borrowings of the Group are mainly recognised in the financial      元借款主要是由本集團以美元作為其功能
statements of the subsidiaries of the Group whose functional currency are       貨幣的附屬公司之財務報表確認，因此涉
USD, the assets and liabilities subject to foreign exchange risks are minimal   及外匯風險的資產與負債皆很小，且抵消
and the relevant risk exposure after offsetting is not significant. As such,    後之風險承擔度亦不重大，故此，人民幣
RMB does not have a significant impact on exchange gains and losses             並沒有對綜合收益表的「其他收益－淨額」
presented on the “Other gains – net” section of the consolidated income         中列報的匯兌收益和虧損一項造成重大的
statement. During the year, the Group did not hedge against its foreign         影響。本年度內，本集團並無對沖其外幣
exchange risks.                                                                 匯率風險。

Regular Board meetings for each year are generally scheduled in advance            每年董事會定期會議的舉行日期一般都會

least 14 days before the meetings. For other Board meetings, reasonable            天前送交全體董事。至於召開其他董事會
notice would be given.                                                             議，將發出合理通知。

meetings, and the Chairman has allowed sufficient time for discussion of           給予充分時間討論，以確保董事會的決定
issues to ensure that Board decisions fairly reflect the consensus of the          能公正反映董事會的共識。
Board.

company secretary. The agenda and the accompanying Board papers are                在董事會定期會議舉行日期不少於3天前
generally circulated to all Directors at least three days before the intended      （其他董事會會議則在儘快切實時間內）送

CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                             主席及行政總裁
Code provision A.2.1 provides that the roles of chairman and chief executive     根據守則條文第A.2.1條，主席與行政總裁
should be separate and should not be performed by the same person.               的角色應予區分，不應由一人同時兼任。
Our Company deviates from this provision because Mr. Tsai Eng-Meng               由於蔡衍明先生擔任主席兼行政總裁兩個
performs both the roles of chairman and chief executive. Mr. Tsai is the         職務，故本公司偏離此條文。蔡先生為本
founder of our Group and has over 40 years of experience in the food and         集團之創辦人，於食品及飲料行業方面擁
beverages industry. Given the current stage of development of our Group,         有逾40年經驗。考慮到本集團目前之發展
the Board believes that vesting the two roles in the same person provides        階段，董事會認為，由一人同時兼任主席
our Company with strong and consistent leadership and facilitates the            與行政總裁，在實施並執行本集團的業務
implementation and execution of our Group’s business strategies. We shall        策略時，可為本公司提供強大而貫徹之領
nevertheless review the structure from time to time in light of the prevailing   導。然而，本集團將根據屆時情況不時檢
circumstances.                                                                   討現行架構。

Board Diversity Policy                                                            董事會成員多元化政策

The Board has adopted a board diversity policy (the “Board Diversity              董事會已採納董事會成員多元化政策（「董
Policy”) with effect from August 2013.                                            事會成員多元化政策」），並於2013年8月起
                                                                                  生效。


The Board reviewed and adopted the revised Board Diversity Policy                 為符合於2019年1月1日起生效的上市規則
effective from 1 December 2018 in order to align with the amendments to           修訂及企業管治守則修訂，董事會檢討並
the Listing Rules and CG Code which came into effect on 1 January 2019.           採納經修訂的董事會成員多元化政策，自
                                                                                  2018年12月1日起生效。


The Board Diversity Policy sets out the approach towards achieving diversity      董事會成員多元化政策旨在載述為達致董
on the Board. In considering the composition of the Board, the Board is of        事會成員多元化而採取的政策方針。在考
the view that diversity can be considered from a number of perspectives,          慮董事會的組成時，董事會認為可以考慮
including but not limited to professional qualifications, regional and industry   不同的多元化因素，包括（但不限於）專業
experience, educational and cultural background, skills, industry knowledge       資歷、區域及行業經驗、教育及文化背
and reputation, gender, ethnicity, language skills and length of service. The     景、技能、行業知識及聲譽、性別、種
above perspectives will be taken into account in determining the optimal          族、語言能力及服務任期。在決定董事會
composition of the Board and where possible, should be balanced among             的最佳組成時，董事會將考慮上述因素，
one another as appropriate. Appointments to the Board will be made based          並於可行的情況下在該等因素之間取得適
on merits and the contributions that the individual is expected to bring to the   當的平衡。在適當地考慮到董事會成員多
Board, with due regard to the benefits of diversity in the Board.                 元化帶來的益處下，董事會成員的委任基
                                                                                  於有關人選的優點長處及預計其將為董事
                                                                                  會帶來的貢獻而作出。


The Nomination Committee monitors the implementation of the Board                 提名委員會持續地監督董事會成員多元化
Diversity Policy on an ongoing basis.                                             政策的執行情況。

•    set out the criteria and process in the nomination and appointment of        •   列明提名及委任本公司董事的條件及
     directors of the Company;                                                        過程；


•    ensure that the Board of the Company has a balance of skills,                •   確保本公司董事會成員具備適合本公

Risk Management and Internal Control                                           風險管理及內部監控
Risk Management and Internal Control Systems of the Group:                     集團風險管理及內部監控系統：
The Board acknowledges that it is responsible for establishing and             董事會承認其須對建立及維持風險管理及
maintaining the Group’s risk management and internal control systems for       內部監控系統負責，並有責任確保該等制
ensuring their effectiveness. Such systems are designed to manage rather       度的有效性。該等系統旨在管理而非消除
than to eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives, and      未能達成業務目標的風險，而且只能就不
can only provide reasonable but not absolute assurance against material        會有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非
misstatement or loss.                                                          絕對的保證。


The Group’s risk management and internal control structure comprises           本集團的風險管理及內部監控架構由董事
the Board, the Audit and Risk Management Committee, the internal audit         會、審核及風險管理委員會、內部審核團
team and the management. To ensure the integrity and effectiveness             隊、以及管理層建立。為保證風險管理及
of risk management and internal controls on an ongoing basis, such             內部監控的完整性及持續有效性，該系
systems closely align with the COSO framework with constant optimization       統與COSO框架保持一致，不斷優化和完
and enhancement. Such systems are designed to achieve the following            善，而建立該體系的目標是：
objectives:


i     providing reasonable assurance of the compliance with relevant rules     i       合理保證業務運作符合相關規則及法
      and regulations of our business operations, and safeguard of assets;             規、資產安全；


ii    ensuring key risks that may impact the Group’s performance are           ii      確保可能影響本集團業績之關鍵風險
      appropriately defined and managed; and                                           已被適當界定及管理；及


iii   ensuring reliable financial accounting records are maintained in         iii     確保根據相關會計準則及監管申報規
      accordance with the relevant accounting standards and regulatory                 定保存可靠的財務會計記錄。
      reporting requirements.

direction of the management and every staff.

RELATIONSHIPS WITH STAKEHOLDERS                                                與利益相關方的關係
The Group believes that its success depends on the support from the            本集團相信成功有賴各利益相關方，包括
stakeholders, including but not limited to employees, customers (primarily     但不限於員工、客戶（主要是經銷商及現代
distributors and modern channel retailers) and end-consumers, suppliers        渠道零售商）及消費者、供應商和股東的支
and shareholders, which is also the key to its sustainable development.        持，對可持續發展而言至關重要。根據本

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                                  股息政策
The Company has adopted a dividend policy (the “Dividend Policy”). It is the     本公司已採納股息政策（「股息政策」）。作為
policy of the Company that, in recommending or declaring dividends, the          本公司一貫的政策，在建議或宣佈股息時，
Board shall make distributions to allow its shareholders to participate in the   本公司董事會應進行派發以允許股東分享其
Company’s profits whilst retaining adequate cash reserves for meeting its        利潤同時維持充足的現金量以滿足其日常營
requirements for working capital and long-term sustainable development.          運資金及長期可持續發展的要求。


The Board has the discretion to propose, declare and distribute dividends        在不違反公司章程細則及一切適用法律與
to the shareholders of the Company, subject to the Articles of Association       法規的前提下、並經考慮以下列明的因
of the Company and all applicable laws and regulations and taking into           素，董事會有酌情權以向本公司股東提
account the factors set out below.                                               議、宣佈及派發股息。


The Board shall take into account the following factors of the Group when        董事會在考慮進行宣佈及支付股息時應計
considering the declaration and payment of dividends:                            及集團的以下因素：


(i)     the financial performance and cash flow situation of the Group;          (i)     本集團之財務表現及現金流狀況；


(ii)    the Group’s business strategies and operations, including future         (ii)    本集團之業務策略及營運，包括未來
        capital requirements and investment needs;                                       對資金需求及投資需要；


(iii)   economic conditions and other internal or external factors that may      (iii)   可能對本集團業務或財務表現及狀況
        have an impact on the business or financial performance and position             產生影響之經濟狀況及其他內部或外
        of the Group;                                                                    部因素；


(iv)    amount of share repurchases made during the period; and                  (iv)    期內進行的股票回購的金額；及


(v)     any other factors that the Board may consider relevant.                  (v)     其他董事會認為相關的因素。


The Company in general meeting may from time to time declare dividends           本公司股東大會可不時向其成員宣佈股
to be paid to the members but no dividends shall be declared in excess of        息，惟其金額不可多於董事會建議的金
the amount recommended by the Board.                                             額。


In addition, the Directors may from time to time pay such interim dividends      除此之外，董事可不時以其認為適宜的金
or special dividends of such amounts and on such dates as they think fit.        額及日期支付中期股息，或特別股息。


The Company may declare and pay dividends by way of cash or warrant or           在不違反公司章程細則的前提下，本公司
by other means that the Board considers appropriate, subject to its Articles     可宣佈及支付現金或認股權證或董事會認
of Association.                                                                  為適宜的其他形式的股息。

1.   GENERAL INFORMATION                                                          1.   一般資料

     Want Want China Holdings Limited (“the Company”) and its                          中國旺旺控股有限公司（「本公司」）及
     subsidiaries (together “the Group”) are principally engaged in the                其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主要從事
     manufacturing and distribution of food and beverages. The Group’s                 製造及分銷食品和飲料。本集團的活
     activities are primarily conducted in the People’s Republic of China              動主要在中華人民共和國（「中國」）進
     (“the PRC”), and its products are also sold to North America, East Asia,          行，其產品亦銷往北美、東亞、東南
     South-East Asia and Europe.                                                       亞及歐洲。

     The Company was incorporated in the Cayman Islands on 3 October                   本公司於2007年10月3日根據開曼群
     2007 as an exempted company with limited liability under the                      島公司法在開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁
     Companies Law of the Cayman Islands. The address of its registered                免有限公司。本公司的註冊辦事處
     office is Maples Corporate Services Limited, P.O. Box 309, Ugland                 地址為Maples Corporate Services
     House, Grand Cayman, KY1-1104, Cayman Islands.                                    Limited, P.O. Box 309, Ugland
                                                                                       House, Grand Cayman, KY1-1104,
                                                                                       Cayman Islands。

     The Company has had its primary listing on the Main Board of The                  本公司股份自2008年3月26日起首次
     Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited since 26 March 2008.                          在香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上
                                                                                       市。

     These financial statements are presented in Renminbi (“RMB”), unless              除另有註明外，此等財務報表均以人
     otherwise stated.                                                                 民幣（「人民幣」）呈列。

2.   SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                   2.   重要會計政策摘要

     The principal accounting policies applied in the preparation of these             編製本綜合財務報表採用的主要會計
     consolidated financial statements are set out below. These policies               政策載於下文。除另有說明外，此等
     have been consistently applied to all the years presented, unless                 政策在所列報的所有年度內貫徹應
     otherwise stated.                                                                 用。

     2.1   Basis of preparation                                                        2.1   編製基準

     The consolidated financial statements of the Group have been                       本集團的綜合財務報表是根據所有適
     prepared in accordance with all applicable Hong Kong Financial                     用的香港財務報告準則（「香港財務報
     Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) and the disclosure requirements of                  告準則」）及香港法例第622章香港公
     the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance Cap. 622. The consolidated                       司條例的披露規定編製。綜合財務報
     financial statements have been prepared under the historical cost                  表按照歷史成本法編製，並就按公平
     convention, as modified by the revaluation of financial assets at fair             值透過其他全面收益記賬的金融資產
     value through other comprehensive income (“FVOCI”) and financial                  （「按公平值透過其他全面收益記賬」）
     assets at fair value through profit or loss (“FVPL”), which are carried at         及按公平值透過損益記賬的金融資產
     fair value.                                                                       （「按公平值透過損益記賬」）的重估
                                                                                       （按公平值計量）而作出修訂。

     The preparation of financial statements in conformity with HKFRSs                 編製符合香港財務報告準則的財務報
     requires the use of certain critical accounting estimates. It also                表需要使用若干關鍵會計估計。這亦
     requires management to exercise its judgement in the process of                   需要管理層在應用本集團的會計政策
     applying the Group’s accounting policies. The areas involving a higher            過程中行使其判斷。涉及高度的判斷
     degree of judgement or complexity, or areas where assumptions and                 或高度複雜性的範疇，或涉及對綜合
     estimates are significant to the consolidated financial statements are            財務報表作出重大假設和估計的範
     disclosed in Note 4.                                                              疇，在附註4中披露。

     2.1    Basis of preparation (continued)                                           2.1       （續）
                                                                                              編製基準

     Changes in accounting policy and disclosures                                      會計政策及披露之變動

     (a)    New standard, amendments and interpretation of HKFRSs                      (a)    本集團於截至2020年3月31日
            adopted by the Group during the year ended 31 March 2020                          止年度已採納香港財務報告準
                                                                                              則的新訂準則、修訂及詮釋

     A number of new standard, amendments and interpretation to existing               多項新訂準則、現有準則的修訂以及
     standards became applicable for the financial year beginning on 1                 詮釋適用於2019年4月1日開始的財政
     April 2019 and the Group had to change its accounting policies as a               年度並且由於採納香港財務報告準則
     result of adopting HKFRS 16 ‘Leases’. The other new amendments                    第16號「租賃」本集團已更改其會計政
     and interpretation did not give rise to any significant impact on the             策。其他新修訂以及詮釋並無對本集
     Group’s financial statements. These new standard, amendments and                  團的財務報表產生任何重大影響。該
     interpretation are set out below:                                                 等新訂準則、修訂及詮釋列示如下：

     (i)    HKFRS 16 ‘Leases’                                                          (i)    香港財務報告準則第16號「租
                                                                                              賃」

            The impact of the adoption of HKFRS 16 ‘Leases’ is disclosed in                   採納香港財務報告準則第16號
            Note 2.2 below.                                                                   「租賃」的影響於下文附註2.2披
                                                                                              露

     (ii)   HK (IFRIC) Interpretation 23 ‘Uncertainty over Income Tax                  (ii)   香 港（國 際 財 務 報 告 詮 釋 委 員
            Treatments’, explains how to recognise and measure deferred                       會）詮釋第23號「所得稅處理之
            and current income tax assets and liabilities where there is                      不確定性」，闡述在存在稅務處
            uncertainty over a tax treatment. In particular, it discusses as                  理不確定性的情況下遞延及當
            below:                                                                            期所得稅資產及負債的確認及
                                                                                              計量方法。具體而言，其就以
                                                                                              下各項進行討論：

            How to determine the appropriate unit of account, and that                        如何確定適當的賬戶單位，及
            each uncertain tax treatment should be considered separately                      須釐定獨立或集中考慮各項不
            or together as a group, depending on which approach better                        確定稅項處理，取決於更佳預
            predicts the resolution of the uncertainty; that the entity should                測不確定性之解決方法；實體
            assume a tax authority will examine the uncertain tax treatments                  應假設稅務機關將檢查不確定
            and have full knowledge of all related information, i.e. that                     的稅務處理並充分了解所有相
            detection risk should be ignored; that the entity should reflect                  關資料，即應忽略檢測風險；
            the effect of the uncertainty in its income tax accounting when it                當稅務機關不太可能接受處理
            is not probable that the tax authorities will accept the treatment;               時，實體應反映其所得稅會計
            that the impact of the uncertainty should be measured using                       中不確定性的影響；應使用最
            either the most likely amount or the expected value method,                       可能發生的金額或期望值法來

            uncertainty; and that the judgements and estimates made must                      決於何種方法可最佳預測不確

                                                                                              響判決，均須重新評估所做出

     2.2     Changes in accounting policies                                           2.2     會計政策變動

     This note explains the impact of the adoption of HKFRS 16 ‘Leases’               本附註解釋採納香港財務報告準則第
     on the Group’s financial statements.                                             16號「租賃」對本集團財務表的影響。

     As indicated in Note 2.1 above, the Group has adopted HKFRS 16                   誠如上文附註2.1所述，本集團自
     ‘Leases’ from 1 April 2019, but has not restated comparatives for the            2019年4月1日起採納香港財務報告準
     financial year ended 31 March 2019, as permitted under the simplified            則第16號「租賃」，唯按該準則的簡化
     transition approach in the standard. The reclassifications and the               過渡方法所允許，並未重列截至2019
     adjustments arising from the new leasing standard are therefore                  年3月31日止財政年度之比較資料。
     recognised in the opening balance sheet on 1 April 2019. The new                 因此，該等新租賃準則所導致的重新
     accounting policies are disclosed in Note 2.26.                                  分類及調整於2019年4月1日的資產負
                                                                                      債表確認。新會計政策於附註2.26內
                                                                                      進行了披露。

     2019. The weighted average lessee’s incremental borrowing rate                   量，並使用承租人於2019年4月1日的

                                                                                            理為短期租賃；

           right-of-use asset at the date of initial application; and                       成本以計量使用權資產；及

     •     the use of hindsight in determining the lease term where the               •     當合約包含延長或終止租約的
           contract contains options to extend or terminate the lease.                      選擇時，以事後分析結果確定
                                                                                            租期。

     2.3     Subsidiaries                                                               2.3     附屬公司

     2.3.1 Consolidation                                                                2.3.1 合併賬目

     A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity) over which the           附屬公司指本集團擁有控制權的所有
     Group has control. The Group controls an entity where the Group is                 主體（包括結構化主體）。當本集團因
     exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with           參與該主體的營運而承擔可變回報的
     the entity and has the ability to affect those returns through its power           風險或享有可變回報的權益並有能力
     over the entity. Subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on which              透過其對該主體的權力影響此等回報
     control is transferred to the Group except for business combination                時，本集團即控制該主體。附屬公司
     under common control as described below. They are deconsolidated                   在控制權轉移至本集團之日起合併
     from the date that control ceases.                                                 入賬（除下述同一控制下企業合併以
                                                                                        外）。附屬公司在控制權終止之日起
                                                                                        停止合併入賬。

     Inter-company transactions, balances, income and expenses on                       集團內公司之間的交易、交易的結
     transactions between group companies are eliminated. Profits and                   餘、收入及開支予以對銷。來自集團
     losses resulting from inter-company transactions that are recognised               內公司間的利潤和損失（確認於資產）
     in assets are also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence             亦予以對銷，除非交易提供轉讓資產
     of an impairment of the transferred asset. Accounting policies                     減值證據則另作別論。附屬公司的會
     of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                        計政策已按需要作出改變，以確保與
     consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.                                本集團採用的政策保持一致。

     2.4   Associates                                                                    2.4   聯營公司

     are accounted for using the equity method of accounting. Under the                  投資以權益法入賬。根據權益法，投
     equity method, the investment is initially recognised at cost, and the              資初步以成本確認，賬面值會增加或
     carry amount is increased or decreased to recognise the investor’s                  減少，以確認投資者佔被投資方收購
     share of the profit or loss of the investee after the date of acquisition.          日期後損益之比例。於收購於聯營公
     Upon the acquisition of the ownership interest in an associate, any                 司之擁有權權益時，聯營公司之成本
     difference between the cost of the associate and the Group’s share of               與本集團應佔聯營公司之可辨認資產
     the net fair value of the associate’s identifiable assets and liabilities is        及負債之公平淨值之任何差額入賬列
     accounted for as goodwill.                                                          作商譽。

     If the ownership interest in an associate is reduced but significant                如聯營公司的權益持有被削減但仍保
     influence is retained, only a proportionate share of the amounts                    留重大影響力，只有按比例將之前在
     previously recognised in other comprehensive income is reclassified                 其他全面收益中確認的數額重新分類
     to profit or loss where appropriate.                                                至損益（如適用）。

     income with a corresponding adjustment to the carrying amount of the                益內確認，並相應調整投資賬面值。

     incurred legal or constructive obligations or made payments on behalf               會確認進一步虧損，除非本集團對聯

     2.18 Borrowings                                                                     2.18 借款

     Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction               借款按公平值並扣除產生的交易費用
     costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently stated at amortised cost;               為初始確認。借款其後按攤銷成本列
     any difference between the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the              賬；所得款（扣除交易成本）與贖回價
     redemption value is recognised in the consolidated income statement                 值的任何差額利用實際利率法於借款
     over the period of the borrowings using the effective interest method.              期間內在綜合收益表確認。

     Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as                 設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部份或
     transaction costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some           全部融資將會很可能提取，該費用確
     or all of the facility will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred        認為貸款的交易費用。在此情況下，
     until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence that it              費用遞延至貸款提取為止。如沒有證
     is probable that some or all of the facility will be drawn down, the fee is         據證明部份或全部融資將會很可能被
     capitalised as a prepayment for liquidity services and amortised over               提取，則該費用資本化作為流動資金
     the period of the facility to which it relates.                                     服務的預付款，並按有關的融資期間
                                                                                         攤銷。

     Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the Group has               除非本集團可無條件將負債的結算遞
     an unconditional right to defer settlement of the liability for at least 12         延至資產負債表日後最少12個月，否則
     months after the balance sheet date.                                                借款分類為流動負債。

     for their intended use or sale, are added to the cost of those assets,              成本，計入該等資產之成本，直至達
     until such time as the assets are substantially ready for their intended            至其預定可使用或出售狀態為止。
     use or sale.

     Investment income earned on the temporary investment of specific                    就特定借款，因有待合資格資產的支

     from the borrowing costs eligible for capitalisation.                               合資格資本化之借款成本中扣除。

     2.20 Current and deferred income tax                                                2.20 當期及遞延所得稅

     The income tax expense for the period comprises current and deferred                本期間所得稅費用包括當期及遞延所
     income tax. Income tax is recognised in the consolidated income                     得稅項。所得稅在綜合收益表中確
     statement, except to the extent that it relates to items recognised in              認，但與在其他全面收益中或直接在

     income tax is also recognised in other comprehensive income or                      該情況下，所得稅亦分別在其他全面
     directly in equity, respectively.                                                   收益或直接在權益中確認。

     (a)   Current income tax                                                            (a)   當期所得稅

     laws enacted or substantively enacted at the balance sheet date in the              司及聯營公司經營及產生應課稅收入
     countries where the Company’s subsidiaries and associates operate                   的國家於資產負債表日已頒佈或實質
     and generate taxable income. Management periodically evaluates                      上已頒佈的稅務法例計算。管理層就
     positions taken in tax returns with respect to situations in which                  適用稅務法例解釋所規限的情況定期
     applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation. It establishes              評估報稅表的狀況，並在適用情況下
     provisions where appropriate on the basis of amounts expected to be                 根據預期須向稅務機關支付的稅款設
     paid to the tax authorities.                                                        定準備。

     (b)   Deferred income tax                                                           (b)   遞延所得稅

     temporary differences arising between the tax bases of assets and                   債的稅基與資產及負債在綜合財務報

     it arises from initial recognition of an asset or liability in a transaction        （不包括業務合併）中對資產或負債的

     affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred income              益或應課稅利潤或損失，則不作記
     tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted                 賬。遞延所得稅採用在資產負債表日
     or substantially enacted by the balance sheet date and are expected                 前已頒佈或實質上已頒佈，並在有關
     to apply when the related deferred income tax asset is realised or the              的遞延所得稅資產實現或遞延所得稅

     rates on government bonds are used.

     The current service cost of the defined benefit plan, recognised in             界定受益計劃的當期服務成本於收益

     from plan amendments or curtailments are recognised immediately in              受益義務的現值變動，會立即在損益
     profit or loss as past service costs.                                           中確認為過往服務成本。

     Past-service costs are recognised immediately in income.                        過往服務成本即時於收益內確認。

     The net interest cost is calculated by applying the discount rate to            淨利息成本採用界定受益責任的淨結
     the net balance of the defined benefit obligation and the fair value of         餘之貼現率及計劃資產的公平值計
     plan assets. This cost is included in employee benefit expense in the           算。此項成本列入收益表的員工福利
     income statement.                                                               開支內。

     2.22 Provisions                                                                   2.22 準備

     Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present legal or                   當本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有
     constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that an        的法律或推定義務；很可能需要有資
     outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation; and the           源的流出以結算義務；及金額已被可
     amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are not recognised for             靠估計，則確認準備。但不會就未來
     future operating losses.                                                          經營虧損確認準備。

     Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an           如有多項類似義務，其需要在結算中
     outflow will be required in settlement is determined by considering the           有資源流出的可能性，則可根據義務
     class of obligations as a whole. A provision is recognised even if the            的類別整體考慮。即使在同一義務類
     likelihood of an outflow with respect to any one item included in the             別所包含的任何一個項目相關的資源
     same class of obligations may be small.                                           流出的可能性極低，仍須確認準備。

     Provisions are measured at the present value of the expenditures                  準備採用稅前利率按照預期需結算有
     expected to be required to settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate             關義務的支出現值計量，該利率反映
     that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money               當時市場對金錢時間值和有關義務固
     and the risks specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision           有風險的評估。隨著時間過去而增加
     due to passage of time is recognised as interest expense.                         的準備確認為利息費用。

     when control of the products has transferred, being when the products            產品的控制權已轉移（即產品交付予

     the channel and price to sell the products, and there is no unfulfilled          渠道及售價及並無可能影響客戶接納
     obligation that could affect the customer’s acceptance of the products.          產品的未履行責任時確認。當產品運
     Delivery occurs when the products have been shipped to the specific              送到指定地點時交付即告完成。當客
     location, the risks of obsolescence and loss have been transferred               戶按照銷售合約接納產品，或接納條
     to the customer, and either the customer has accepted the products               款已失效，或本集團有客觀證據證明
     in accordance with the sales contract, the acceptance provisions                 所有接納標準均已達成時，產品毀損
     have lapsed, or the Group has objective evidence that all criteria for           及遺失之風險轉由客戶承擔。
     acceptance have been satisfied.

     The products are often sold with retrospective volume discounts                  產品通常以一段時期內的銷售總量為
     based on aggregate sales over a period of time. Revenue from these               基準，進行追溯批量折扣銷售。該等
     sales is recognised based on the price specified in the contract, net of         銷售的收益乃基於合約規定的價格，
     the estimated volume discounts. Accumulated experience is used to                經扣除估計批量折扣後確認。本公司
     estimate and provide for the discounts, and revenue is only recognised           使用累積的經驗估計及提供折扣，且
     to the extent that it is highly probable that a significant reversal will        收益僅於重大撥回極大可能不會產生
     not occur. A refund liability (included in accruals and other payables)          時確認。當預期向客戶應付有關銷售
     is recognised for expected volume discounts payable to customers in              的批量折扣時確認退款責任（包括於
     relation to sales. No element of financing is deemed present as the              應計費用及其他應付款內）。由於銷
     sales are made with a credit term of 60-90 days, which is consistent             售之信用期為60日至90日，符合市場
     with market practice.                                                            慣例，故並不存在融資因素。

     the passage of time is required before the payment is due.                       流逝，故收取對價成為無條件。

     2.25 Interest income                                                                2.25 利息收入

     Interest income is calculated by applying the effective interest rate               利息收入是通過對金融資產的賬面總
     to the gross carrying amount of a financial asset except for financial              額按實際利率來計算的，但信用減損
     assets that subsequently become credit-impaired. For credit-impaired                的金融資產除外。對於信用減損的金
     financial assets, the effective interest rate is applied to the net carrying        融資產，實際利率適用於該金融資產
     amount of the financial asset (after deduction of the loss allowance).              的帳面淨額（扣除虧損撥備後）。

     Interest income is presented as finance income where it is earned                   利息收入呈列為持作現金管理用途的
     from financial assets that are held for cash management purposes,                   金融資產所賺取的財務收入，見下文
     see Note 28 below. Any other interest income is included in other                   附註28。任何其他利息收入均被計入
     income.                                                                             其他收入。

     2.26 Leases                                                                         2.26 租賃

     As explained in Note 2.2 above, the Group has changed its accounting                如上文附註2.2所述，本集團已變更
     policy for leases where the Group is the lessee. The new policy is                  其作為承租人的租賃會計政策。新政
     described below and the impact of the change in Note 2.2.                           策於下文載述，而有關變動的影響載
                                                                                         於附註2.2。

     Until 31 March 2019, leases in which a significant portion of the risks             直至2019年3月31日，出租人保留所
     and rewards of ownership were retained by the lessor were classified as             有權之大部分風險及回報之租賃均分
     operating leases. Payments made under operating leases (net of any                  類為經營租賃。根據經營租賃支付之
     incentives received from the lessor) were charged to the consolidated               款項（扣除自出租人收取之任何獎勵
     income statement on a straight-line basis over the period of the lease.             金後），於租賃期內以直線法在綜合
                                                                                         收益表內列作開支。

     From 1 April 2019, leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset                    自2019年4月1日起，租賃確認為使用
     and a corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset is              權資產，並在租賃資產可供本集團使
     available for use by the Group.                                                     用之日確認相應負債。

     2.26 Leases (continued)                                                               （續）
                                                                                     2.26 租賃

     Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured on a         租賃產生的資產及負債初步按現值計
     present value basis. Lease liabilities include the net present value of         量。租賃負債包括固定付款（包括實
     fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments).                         質固定付款）的淨現值。

     Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension                    根據合理確定的延期選擇支付的租賃
     options are also included in the measurement of the liability.                  款也包括在負債計量中。

     The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in           租賃付款額按照租賃內含利率貼現。
     the lease. If that rate cannot be readily determined, which is generally        如果無法確定該利率（此情況普遍存
     the case for leases in the Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing            在於本集團租賃中），則應採用承租
     rate is used, being the rate that the individual lessee would have to           人的增量借款利率，即個別承租人為
     pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of similar value           在類似經濟環境下獲得價值相近的資
     to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment with similar        產，以類似條款及條件借入資金而必
     terms, security and conditions.                                                 須支付的利率。

     To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                         為釐定遞增借款利率，本集團：

     •    where possible, uses recent third-party financing received by the          •   在可能情況下，使用個別承租
          individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to reflect changes             人最近獲得的第三方融資為出
          in financing conditions since third party financing was received               發點作出調整以反映自獲得第
                                                                                         三方融資以來融資條件的變動

     •    uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free interest rate        •   使用累加法，首先就本集團所
          adjusted for credit risk for leases held by the Group, which does              持有租賃的信用風險（最近並無
          not have recent third party financing; and                                     第三方融資）調整無風險利率；
                                                                                         及

     •    makes adjustments specific to the lease, eg term, country,                 •   進行特定於租約的調整，例如
          currency and security.                                                         期限、國家、貨幣及抵押。

     Lease payments are allocated between principal and finance cost. The            租賃付款於本金及融資成本之間作出
     finance cost is charged to profit or loss over the lease period so as to        分配。融資成本在租賃期間於損益扣
     produce a constant periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance           除，藉以令各期間的債餘額的期間利
     of the liability for each period.                                               率一致。

3.   FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                     3.   財務風險管理

     3.1   Financial risk factors                                                       3.1   財務風險因素

     The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks: market           本集團的活動承受著多種的財務風

     3.2   Capital risk management                                                   3.2   資本風險管理

     The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the               本集團的資本管理政策，是保障本集
     Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to provide              團能繼續經營，以為股東提供回報和
     returns for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders and to             為其他利益關係者提供利益，同時維
     maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.            持最佳的資本結構以減低資本成本。

     In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may             為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可
     adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, return capital to          能會調整支付予股東的股息數額、向
     shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets to reduce debts.                  股東退還資本、發行新股或出售資產
                                                                                     以減低債務。

     Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors capital              與業內其他公司一樣，本集團利用淨
     on the basis of the net gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as total        權益負債比率監察其資本。此比率按
     borrowings net of cash and cash equivalents divided by total equity             已扣除現金及現金等價物的總借款除
     excluding non-controlling interests.                                            以總權益（不含非控制性權益）計算。

     The net gearing ratios at 31 March 2020 and 31 March 2019 were as               於2020年3月31日及2019年3月31
     follows:                                                                        日，淨權益負債率如下：

     3.3    Fair value estimation                                                        3.3     公平值估計

     The table below analyses the Group’s financial instruments carried                  下表根據在評估公平值的估值技術中
     at fair value as at 31 March 2020 by level of the inputs to valuation               所運用到的輸入的層級，分析本集團
     techniques used to measure fair value. Such inputs are categorised                  於2020年3月31日按公平值入賬的金
     into three levels within a fair value hierarchy as follows:                         融工具。這些輸入按照公平值層級歸
                                                                                         類為如下三層：

     •      Quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for identical assets            •       相同資產或負債在活躍市場的
            or liabilities (level 1).                                                            報價（未經調整）
                                                                                                        （第1層）。

     •      Inputs other than quoted prices included within level 1 that are             •       除了第1層所包括的報價外，該
            observable for the asset or liability, either directly (that is, as                  資產或負債的可觀察的其他輸
            prices) or indirectly (that is, derived from prices) (level 2).                      入可為直接（即例如價格）或間
                                                                                                 接（即源自價格）

     (a)   Financial instruments in Level 1                                              (a)     第1層金融工具

     The fair value of financial instruments traded in active markets is                 在活躍市場買賣的金融工具的公平值
     based on quoted market prices at the balance sheet date. A market is                根據資產負債表日的市場報價列賬。
     regarded as active if quoted prices are readily and regularly available             當報價可即時和定期從證券交易所獲
     from an exchange, and the price represents actual and regularly                     得，而該報價代表按公平交易基準進
     occurring market transactions on an arm’s length basis. The quoted                  行的實際和常規市場交易時，該市場
     market price used for financial assets held by the Group is the current             被視為活躍。本集團持有的金融資產
     bid price. These instruments are included in level 1.                               的市場報價為當時買方報價。此等工
                                                                                         具包括在第1層。

4.   CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                           4.   重大會計估計及判斷
     JUDGEMENTS

     Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated and are based on              估計及判斷會持續評估，並按過往
     historical experience and other factors, including expectations of future        經驗及其他因素（包括於有關情況下
     events that are believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.               相信為合理之未來事件之預測）而作
                                                                                      出。

     The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the future.                 本集團對未來作出估計及假設。所得
     The resulting accounting estimates will, by definition, seldom equal             的會計估計如其定義，很少會與其實
     the related actual results. The estimates and assumptions that have              際結果相同。很大機會導致下個財政
     a significant risk of causing a material adjustment to the carrying              年度的資產及負債的賬面值作出重大
     amounts of assets and liabilities within the next financial year are             調整的估計及假設討論如下。
     addressed below.

     PRC taxes                                                                        中國稅項

     The Group is mainly subject to different taxes in the PRC. Significant           本集團主要須繳納中國不同稅項。於
     judgement is required in determining the provision for income taxes.             釐定所得稅撥備時，須作出重大判
     There are some transactions and calculations for which the ultimate              斷。於日常業務過程中，有眾多交易
     tax determination is uncertain during the ordinary course of business.           及計算是無法肯定最終的稅務決定。
     The Group recognizes liabilities for anticipated tax audit issues based          本集團根據是否估計將有額外的稅項
     on estimates of whether additional taxes will be due. Where the final            到期而確認所預計稅務審核事宜的責
     tax outcome of these matters is different from the amounts that are              任。凡該等事宜的最終稅務結果有別
     initially recorded, such differences will impact the current income              於初步記錄的金額，該等差異將影響
     tax and deferred income tax provisions in the period in which such               作出有關決定的期間的當期所得稅稅
     determination is made.                                                           項及遞延所得稅稅項撥備。

10. LEASES (continued)                                                         10. 租賃
                                                                                    （續）

    (iii)   The Group’s leasing activities and how these are accounted for       (iii)   本集團的租賃活動及其會計方
                                                                                         法

    The Group leases various leasehold land and land use rights, offices,        本集團租賃各種租賃土地及土地使用
    warehouses and equipment. Rental contracts are typically made                權、辦公室、倉庫及設備。租約一般
    for fixed periods. Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis         為固定期。租賃條款按個別基準協商
    and contain a wide range of different terms and conditions. The              且包含多種不同條款及條件。租賃協
    lease agreements do not impose any covenants, but except for the             議並無施加任何條款，除租賃土地及
    leasehold land and land use rights, leased assets may not be used as         土地使用權外的租賃資產不得用作借
    security for borrowing purposes.                                             款的擔保品。

    (iv)    Extension and termination options                                    (iv)    延期及終止選擇權

    Extension and termination options are included in a number of                本集團的若干物業及設備租賃包含延
    property and equipment leases across the Group. These terms are              期及終止選擇權。該等條款乃用於就
    used to maximise operational flexibility in terms of managing contracts.     管理合約令經營靈活性最大化。所持
    The majority of extension and termination options held are exercisable       有的大部分延期及終止選擇權僅可由
    only by the Group and not by the respective lessor.                          本集團行使，唯不得由有關出租人行
                                                                                 使。

    (c)   Statutory reserves                                                       (c)   法定儲備

    The PRC laws and regulations require companies registered in                   中國法律法規要求在中國註冊的公司
    the PRC to provide for certain statutory reserves, which are to be             從各自法定財務報表所呈報的淨利潤
    appropriated from the net profit (after offsetting accumulated losses          （抵銷以往年度的累計虧損後）中，於
    from prior years) as reported in their respective statutory financial          分派利潤予權益持有人前，劃撥款項
    statements, before profit distributions to equity holder. All statutory        以提撥若干法定儲備金。所有法定儲
    reserves are created for specific purposes. PRC companies are                  備金均為特別用途而設立。中國公司
    required to appropriate 10% of statutory net profits to statutory surplus      於分派當年度的除稅後利潤，須向法
    reserves, upon distribution of their post-tax profits of the current year.     定盈餘儲備劃撥淨利潤的10%。當公
    A company may discontinue the contribution when the aggregate sum              司法定盈餘儲備的總和超過其註冊資
    of the statutory surplus reserve is more than 50% of its registered            本的50%時，公司可停止劃撥。法定
    capital. The statutory surplus reserves shall only be used to make             盈餘儲備只可用作彌補公司的虧損、
    up losses of the companies, to expand the companies’ production                擴大公司的生產營運或增加公司的資
    operations, or to increase the capital of the companies. In addition,          本。此外，按照董事會的決議案，公
    a company may make further contribution to the discretional surplus            司可使用其除稅後利潤酌情向盈餘儲
    reserve using its post-tax profits in accordance with resolutions of the       備作出進一步供款。
    Board of Directors.

    The Taiwan Company Law requires a company to appropriate 10%                   「台灣地區公司法」規定公司於宣派
    of its annual net income to legal reserve before it declares any part of       任何部分的年度淨收入作為股息及╱
    such net income as dividends and/or bonuses, until the accumulated             或紅利前，將其年度淨收入的10%劃
    legal reserve equals the total registered capital. This reserve can only       撥為法定儲備，直至累計法定儲備等
    be used to cover losses, or, if the balance of the reserve exceeds             於總註冊資本為止。該儲備只可用作
    50% of the registered capital, to increase the registered capital by an        彌補虧損，或假若該儲備的結餘超逾
    amount not exceeding 50% of the legal reserve.                                 註冊資本的50%，則可用作增加註
                                                                                   冊資本，惟數額不得超過法定儲備的
                                                                                   50%。

    Deferred income tax assets are recognised for tax losses carry-forward                  很有可能透過未來應繳稅利潤而

36. BUSINESS COMBINATION                                                      36. 業務合併

   Acquisition of Poyang County Linwang Forestry Development Co.,               收購鄱陽縣林旺林業開發有限公司
   Limited

   In June 2019, Want Want (HK) Holdings Limited, a subsidiary of               於2019年6月，本公司的子公司香港
   the Company, entered into a share purchase agreement with San                旺旺控股有限公司與神旺控股有限公
   Want Holdings Limited, pursuant to which, Want Want (HK) Holdings            司簽訂了股權收購協定，根據該協
   Limited acquired Poyang County Linwang Forestry Development Co.,             定，香港旺旺控股有限公司從神旺控
   Limited (hereinafter referred to as “Poyang Linwang”) from San Want          股有限公司收購了鄱陽縣林旺林業開
   Holdings Limited at a total consideration of USD7.35 million. In the         發有限公司（「鄱陽林旺」），總對價為
   opinion of the directors of the Company, Poyang Linwang carries on a         735萬美元。本公司董事認為，鄱陽
   business. Before and after the acquisition, Want Want (HK) Holdings          林旺經營一項業務。收購前後，香港
   Limited and San Want Holdings Limited were ultimately controlled             旺旺控股有限公司及神旺控股有限公
   by Mr. Tsai Eng-Meng, and the control was not temporary. As such,            司均由蔡衍明先生最終控制，控制並
   the acquisition is considered to be a business combination under             非暫時性的。因此，收購被視為共同
   common control. According to Hong Kong Accounting Guideline 5                控制下的企業合併。根據香港會計師
   “Merger Accounting for Common Control Combination” issued by the             公會發出的香港會計指引第5號「同一
   Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants, the comparative         控制下合併會計處理」，本集團綜合
   financial figures of the consolidated balance sheet, consolidated            資產負債表、綜合權益變動表、綜合
   statement of changes in equity, consolidated cash flow statement and         現金流量表及相關附註的比較財務數
   related explanatory notes of the Group were restated as if Poyang            字進行了重列，猶如鄱陽林旺自2008
   Linwang had been combined since the date of establishment in                 年成立之日起就已納入合併。本集團
   2008. The comparative financial figures of the consolidated income           綜合收益表及綜合全面收益表的比較
   statement and consolidated statement of comprehensive income of              財務數字因金額不重大未進行重列。
   the Group were not restated as the amount was insignificant.

   The carrying amounts of the assets and liabilities of Poyang Linwang         於交易日期鄱陽林旺之資產及負債賬
   as at the transaction date and the comparative financial figures were as     面值及比較財務數字載列如下：
   follows:
